```
- Next action: <exact next step or next task id>
```

#### ESCALATION — GT-03

Blocking condition: The prescribed locale-only phrase additions cannot produce the approved Hindi Use label, and Archived's actual shared translation key differs from the spec's resolver/value; changing shared mappings would affect text beyond this tour's scope.

Specification: `tasks/GT-03-saved-costings.md:82`: “a resolver's live label differs from the spec's Gujarati/Hindi value in a way that would change another page's text”. Page spec §F.2 (`pages/saved-costings.md:159-170`) requires the listed native values and the same resolver as the live control; its note correctly directs using an existing `t('key')` where present. The allowed surface includes new locale entries, not global phrase-map precedence or `base-plan-phrases.ts`. Plan LR-02/LR-03 require control/tour label parity.

Source evidence: `QuotesClient.tsx:1334` renders Archived through `t('common.archived')`; locale `gu.ts:332` and `hi.ts:332` contain the shorter values recorded above. Its Use buttons at `:581` and `:608` call `tx('Use')`. `lib/i18n/context.tsx:107,123-130` loads locale strings first, then overrides with generated/legacy/Base phrases; `base-plan-phrases.ts:519` fixes Hindi Use to `उपयोग करें`, overriding any new locale phrase. The same global map controls generic Archived statuses (`components/ui/page-header.tsx:146`); Base Hindi Archived at `:258` is `आर्काइव किए गए`. Probe output and screenshots are preserved at the paths above.

Expected: Tour tokens and their live controls display exactly the approved §F.2 values in each language, without changing unrelated shared labels or the localization engine.

Actual: The verified current labels differ. Adding only `tourControls.savedCostings.use` to the locales while retaining `tx('Use')` will be overwritten; changing `common.archived` or the global Archived phrase to match the table changes shared text. Switching just the tour's resolver would leave it inconsistent with the live controls.

Tried: Confirmed exact render sites, traced dictionary precedence and shared consumers, then ran only the targeted Gujarati/Hindi diagnostic in the existing stub harness. Evaluated a feature-scoped key solution; no application edit or acceptance waiver applied.

Why this exceeds my discretion: The packet explicitly makes this localization conflict an escalation condition. Changing the shared phrase table/precedence is outside its allowed surface; keeping different labels without a decision would change approved copy. The safe proposed alternative changes the locked resolver wiring for the two controls.

Smallest decision needed: Authorize using `t('tourControls.savedCostings.use')` and `t('tourControls.savedCostings.archived')` in the Saved Costings live controls and corresponding tour resolver, with the existing §F.2 values (Use: en `Use`, gu `વાપરો`, hi `इस्तेमाल करें`; Archived: en `Archived`, gu `આર્કાઇવ કરેલી`, hi `आर्काइव की गई`), leaving global phrase overrides and `common.archived` unchanged? All other tokens keep their actual live resolver or the packet's specified phrase-addition path.

Worktree state: only this ledger modified and ignored diagnostic evidence added; GT-03 implementation and acceptance checks UNVERIFIED. GT-00/01/02 PASSED, no GT-03 commit/push/PR, server stopped. Resume at the label decision and remaining packet reads; do not proceed to GT-04.

The control table at lines 89–96 has only activateQuality, assignLooms, manageWorkers, machineBoard, switchQuality and qualityActions. `EXECUTOR_PROMPT.md:63`: “Gujarati and Hindi copy comes from the page specs; do not invent or machine-translate new copy. If a string you need is missing from the spec, escalate.”

**Source evidence:** `BaseMachineBoard.tsx:634–635` has a literal `Manage Operators for {managingMachine?.custom_label || managingMachine?.name}`, no live translation key. `BaseActiveQualities.tsx:508` and `:595` render literal `Cost:`. `ProductionTourPreview.tsx:42` currently uses `heading('Manage Workers')`; `:26` uses `tx('Production cost')`. Existing `Cost` matches are generated legacy translation entries (`legacy-generated.ts:289,1415`), not the approved page-spec copy or the required shared live key. The preview heading helper uses `tx` (`:19`); `context.tsx:458–460` resolves that through the phrase/fallback translator, which cannot supply missing approved copy by authority.

**Actual:** No approved Gujarati/Hindi title/cost rows or existing operators-title key are available. Blocking condition: Required Gujarati/Hindi copy for the retained calculation-help dialog and sibling Payroll controls is absent from the fixed page specification.
